Then they'd time out with P≈0 → our demon score ≈ 595 - cost penalty. Meanwhile, a naive hero dies instantly with D=100 → 1000.

Alternatively a map that gives them a "way" that kills them: the ring is visible, but just beyond the ring we place ice etc. Hmm, if they can't cross the ring at all, they never die.

Better: the "trap" is a juicy-looking path. Give them a visible safe route (EMPTY cells) that leads through a "CHEST" (MIMIC) gauntlet into a laser corridor etc. Since it's visible as safe EMPTY, they'll take it eagerly. That's what gets competent heroes: a route that LOOKS safe but isn't.

Concretely: the safest-looking route (all EMPTY) from spawn to the keys, but:

Multiple MIMICs (masked as CHEST) placed exactly on the唯一 corridor — forced -45 each.
CRACK tiles that collapse after leaving — visible as CRACK; a competent hero might still step (they're safe to stand on, only unsafe if you linger... no: they collapse on leaving, so as you leave it becomes PIT; that's fine, you're leaving. Wait, it's dangerous: "当勇者或 BOX 离开该格的瞬间，立即永久坍塌变为 PIT". If the hero leaves, the tile becomes a PIT behind them. That's only dangerous if they need to come back. So CRACK is not immediately lethal — unless there's a chain: e.g., you step onto a CRACK and then the ICE carries you... no. CRACK+danger arises when you leave a CRACK and then something pushes you back. Or when standing on CRACK and an explosion collapses it.
So the real killers: MIMIC (45), PIT (−100), lasers (50 each), TNT (60), BOMB (50), falling into pit after explosion.

So pile on: a forced corridor with 2 MIMICs (90 damage) + 1 laser hit (50) = 140 > 100 HP → death. But they have 100 HP; 140 kills. If they also get a HEAL chest (+30), it nets differently. Hmm.

But also, our author_solution must survive the same forced corridor. If the corridor is forced past 2 MIMICs, our hero takes 90 damage too, then dies? Unless our author solution picks up HEALs. Interesting: we can design so our own hero heals. Hmm, HEAL restores 30. So our author solution could: take 2 mimics (-90), then a HEAL (+30) = 40 HP, then reach V. Total damage dealt D = min(100, 90) = 90. Ok. But a MIMIC's damage counts toward D as "机关伤害" (yes, trap damage). Hmm, and D caps at 100.

Alternatively, we want D=100 for the kill bonus. If the kill happens with 100 damage, D=100. So we want the total effective trap damage to reach 100 before death. For a hero with 100 HP and no heals, a death always has D=100 (since hp goes ≤ 0, total damage ≥ 100 and capped at 100).

So D=100 on any kill, assuming no healing. Easy.

So the design question reduces to: maximize kill probability and minimize turn-of-death (for 50*(3-P)) and cost g.

OK, key insight: In BLIND mode with a 3-tile radius, any static hazard is visible before you can reach it (you see 3 ahead; you move 1 per turn; so you always see the hazard 2 turns before you'd step on it — unless it's around a blind corner or hidden behind a wall).

MIMIC: visible as CHEST, clue reveals it → avoidable if clue-aware.
Falling: CRACK collapsing under you (if you stand on CRACK when an explosion hits, you fall). Visible CRACK.
Boxes: pushing a box can slide it and... the box itself doesn't hurt the hero; the CRACK under the box does.
Gates flipping (SWITCH): a gate closes and... no damage, but it can block retreat.
So the two big kill mechanisms: momentum traps and lasers. Both are visible-ish but with blind outcomes (momentum) or forced exposure (lasers).

"""Faithful re-implementation of the RULES.md v2.1 judge engine."""

W, H = 40, 26

HIGH_WALLS = {"W", "T_W", "LASER_U", "LASER_D", "LASER_L", "LASER_R"}

COSTS = {...}

class Game:
    def __init__(self, grid, seed=...):
        validate grid shape
        decode 4 layers
        ...
Key details:

Decoding:

terrain[y][x] = "EMPTY" if grid cell in ("BOX","TNT") else grid[y][x]
occupant[y][x] = grid[y][x] if in ("BOX","TNT") else None
bombs = []
clues: row-major for each MIMIC (mx,my): check U,D,L,R neighbors in that order, first that satisfies:
terrain EMPTY and occupant None
not in spawn zone x∈[1,3], y∈[12,14]
the candidate's orthogonal 4 neighbors contain no other chest (HEAL or MIMIC), i.e., excluding the current (mx,my)
clues[ny][nx] not yet used → mark clues[ny][nx] = True and bind it to the mimic. If none → invalid layout.
Wait, careful about condition 3: "该格 (nx, ny) 的正交四邻中不存在任何其他宝箱（即除了当前 (mx, my) 外，不与任何其他 HEAL 或 MIMIC 正交相邻）". So for the clue cell (nx,ny), its 4 neighbors must not include any HEAL/MIMIC other than the current mimic. Hmm, "不存在任何其他宝箱（即除了当前 (mx, my) 外...）" — so the clue cell's neighbors may include the current mimic only. Since (nx,ny) is adjacent to (mx,my) by construction, the condition is: among the clue cell's 4 neighbors, the only chest is (mx,my).

Pseudo:

代码块


def atomic(entity, p0, p1, dir, ctx):
    # returns 'continue' with new ctx state, or 'stop', or 'dead'/'cleared'
    ctx.steps += 1
    # 2. leave hook
    if terrain[p0] == 'CRACK': terrain[p0] = 'PIT'
    # 3. update coords
    if entity == 'BOX':
        occ[p0] = None; occ[p1] = 'BOX'
    else:
        hero.x, hero.y = p1
        exposed.add(p1)
    # 4. arrive hook
    t = terrain[p1]
    if entity == 'BOX':
        if t == 'PIT': occ[p1]=None; terrain[p1]='EMPTY'; ctx.stop=True  (momentum terminated for this box)
        elif t == 'MIMIC': terrain[p1]='EMPTY'; clear clue
        elif t == 'SWITCH': gates_flipped ^= 1
    else:
        if t == 'PIT': hp -= 100; add_trap_damage(100)
        elif t == 'MIMIC': hp -= 45; add trap 45; terrain='EMPTY'; clear clue; ctx.stop_momentum=True
        elif t == 'HEAL': hp = min(100, hp+30); terrain='EMPTY'
        elif t == 'SWITCH': gates_flipped ^= 1
    # 5. death
    if entity == 'HERO' and hp <= 0: status='DEAD'; return 'terminate'
    # 6. keys / vault
    if entity == 'HERO':
        if t == 'K1': has_k1 = True; terrain='EMPTY'
        elif t == 'K2': has_k2 = True; terrain='EMPTY'
        elif t == 'V' and has_k1 and has_k2 and hp>0: status='CLEARED'; return 'terminate'
    # 7. step cap / momentum stop
    if ctx.steps >= 32 or ctx.stop_momentum: return 'stop'
    # 8. momentum continuation based on terrain[p1]
    ...
Careful about the PIT damage accounting for the hero: -100 damage counted as trap damage (min(h,100)). In the "add trap damage" bookkeeping: ΔD = min(hp_before, damage) — the rules say the damage is applied then hp reduced; and D accumulates min(hp, damage). Let's implement add: eff = min(max(hp,0), dmg) then hp -= dmg; D += eff. Careful: for the PIT case, hp_after = hp-100 → hp_before=100 → eff=100.

Hmm, careful for the "MIMIC" case: damage 45, eff = min(hp, 45).

For the explosion bookkeeping (phase C step 4): a = trap damage total (TNT 60 per source), b = self-damage (BOMB 50 per source). eff = min(h, a+b) * a/(a+b) if a+b>0.

For lasers: eff = min(hp, 50m); hp -= 50m.

For the post-explosion fall (standing on a PIT after the explosion): "勇者立即落坑受到 100 点机关伤害（记入有效机关伤害并 hp -= 100）" → eff = min(hp, 100).

Step 8: momentum continuation:

ICE: keep dir, check next cell; if enterable → recurse; else stop.
MIRROR: turn dir 90°, then same as ICE.
SPRING: ...
PORTAL (and not just arrived from the pair): look up the pair; check if it's enterable (occ None, and for a BOX p_pair != hero pos); if not enterable → stay at p1, stop; if enterable → teleport, portal_count += 1, do steps 1-7 from p1 → p_pair; at p_pair after step 8: if portal_count >= 2 → stop at p_pair; if portal_count == 1 → keep dir and try to step 1 normal cell forward (if enterable → enter and continue per the next cell's terrain; else stop at p_pair).
The suppress_portal_once flag: when the entity arrives at a portal's pair, it should not immediately re-teleport back. If the pair end is itself a portal (it is by definition), then continuing to interpret step 8 for the arrival cell would teleport back. So when we teleport from p1 to p_pair, we set suppress_portal_once=True and process step 8 at p_pair with the portal branch skipped. What does "改按下方门后迈步规则处理" mean — "the door-step rule below"? Hmm, that phrase likely refers to the portal branch's own logic: "若 portal_count == 1，实体保持进入方向 dir 尝试向 p_pair 前方相邻格迈出 1 格普通位移" — i.e., for the arrival at p_pair, we perform the "step forward 1 cell" logic. So arriving at the pair end with portal_count==1 → step 1 forward; with portal_count>=2 → stop.

So effectively: after teleporting (portal_count += 1), we're at p_pair; skip the portal re-teleport logic (suppress) and apply: if portal_count >= 2 → stop; else step 1 forward (normal move, recursion from p_pair to p_next) and then continue normal momentum.

The explosion closure BFS:

queue Q = sources (each a position with a kind: BOMB or TNT)
Process: for each source, its 3x3 area; any other unexploded TNT (occ == 'TNT') or unexploded BOMB within that area → add to Q (chain).
Then damage: count sources covering the hero: sum a (TNT 60) and b (BOMB 50).
Delta D = min(h, a+b)*a/(a+b) if a+b>0; hp -= (a+b).
Remove exploded BOMBs (from the bombs list) and exploded TNTs (occ → None for those cells).
For all cells in the union of the 3x3 areas: if occ == BOX → occ=None; terrain changes per the table (T_W/K1/K2/V/PORTAL/PIT immune; CRACK→PIT; others→EMPTY).
Post-explosion support check: any surviving BOX whose terrain is PIT → box removed, terrain EMPTY. If hp>0 and terrain[hero] == PIT → hero takes 100 (D += min(hp,100); hp -= 100).
If hp <= 0 → dead.

代码块


def _entity_move_chain(self, kind, x0,y0, x1,y1, dkey, steps, portal_count):
    # atomic displacement x0->x1, then momentum continuation
    while True:
        steps += 1
        # leave hook
        if self.terrain[y0][x0] == "CRACK": self.terrain[y0][x0] = "PIT"
        # apply
        if kind == "BOX":
            self.occ[y0][x0] = None; self.occ[y1][x1] = "BOX"
        else:
            self.hero["x"], self.hero["y"] = x1, y1
            self.exposed.add((x1,y1))
        # arrive hooks
        term = self._arrive(kind, x1, y1)
        if term == "stop_all": return (x1,y1,steps,portal_count,"stopped")
        if term == "dead": return (x1,y1,steps,portal_count,"dead")
        if term == "cleared": return (...)
        if steps >= 32: return (...)  # step 7
        if self.stop_momentum_flag: ... 
        # momentum
        t = self.terrain[y1][x1]
        ...
        x0,y0 = x1,y1; x1,y1 = next
Careful: stop_momentum = True is set by the hero's MIMIC arrival → terminates the chain (step 7). I'll return immediately after the arrive hook in that case.

Let me write.

python


def _damage(self, dmg, is_self=False):
    h = self.hero["hp"]
    eff = min(max(h,0), dmg)
    self.hero["hp"] = h - dmg
    if not is_self:
        self.trap_damage += eff
    return eff

Let me enumerate the possible "hatch" cells adjacent to the zone and their properties:

T_W/W/laser: HIGH_WALL → blocks everything; W is bomb-destroyable; T_W/laser... lasers are bomb-destroyable too (a bomb can't reach them: distance ≥2; but a TNT blast could).
PIT: deadly to step on; jumpable over.
TNT: blocks movement; jumpable over (p1 not a wall) if p2 is free; bomb-blast can arm it (chain → destroys nearby W).
BOX: blocks movement; pushable; jumpable over if p2 is free.
CRACK: passable (a hole) ✗.
Gates: R_GATE (closed initially) = HIGH_WALL with a global switch; B_GATE = passable ✗.
So the hatch options that are not a simple walk-through: PIT (jump over), TNT (jump over / detonate), BOX (push / jump over), W (bomb / TNT blast).

Our author needs a way out. The cheapest for the author: JUMP over a PIT (1 action, from the zone) or JUMP over a TNT (1 action) or push a BOX (1 action) or bomb a W (3+ actions).

代码块


def _breach_decision(state, known, occ_m, flipped, hx, hy, goal, hp, bombs, memory):
    # returns an action or None
Steps:

Compute the reachable set from (hx,hy) over known-passable cells (BFS, movement only, no jumps).
Find candidate W cells: known terrain == "W" (destroyable) adjacent to the reachable set. (Also could be other destroyable tiles, but W is the common wall.) Score by manhattan to the goal; pick the best.
If none → return None.
Determine the bomb cell: a reachable cell b with chebyshev(b, w) <= 1; prefer b == (hx,hy) if adjacent; else the closest b.
If (hx,hy) == b:
Check: is there a reachable cell at chebyshev ≥2 from b (for retreating)? If yes → return "BOMB".
Else → try another b? or return None.
Else: move along the BFS path to b → return the first move action.
Also: don't breach if we already have a live bomb of ours (to avoid stacking)? Fine — the state's bombs include ours; if any bomb exists with x,y == our cell... hmm. Let me also require: no existing bomb within chebyshev 1 of b.

Memory: store nothing (stateless recompute each turn) ✓ simpler. Hmm, but there's a risk of oscillation: if we place a bomb and then the next turn the breach logic says "move to b" again (we're at b) → "BOMB" again → hmm, the game's BOMB rule: if a bomb already exists at our cell, BOMB is a no-op (WAIT-equivalent) — so it's harmless but wastes turns. Let me handle: if a bomb already exists at our cell → don't BOMB; instead retreat (move toward a cell at chebyshev ≥2 from the bomb) ✓. The hot logic already penalizes staying → the normal planner would move. But if we're "stuck", the planner may have no good move... Let me implement: in the breach function, if a bomb exists at our cell (or within 1) → return a move away (the BFS toward a cell at distance ≥2), or WAIT if there's nowhere to go.

python


def _bfs_reach(start, known, occ_m, flipped):
    """reachable passable cells from start (movement only)."""
    seen = {start}
    q = [start]
    order = []
    while q:
        u = q.pop()
        order.append(u)
        ux = u % GRID_W; uy = u // GRID_W
        for dk in DIR_KEYS:
            dx, dy = DIRS[dk]
            nx = ux+dx; ny = uy+dy
            if not (0 <= nx < GRID_W and 0 <= ny < GRID_H): continue
            v = ny*GRID_W+nx
            if v in seen: continue
            t = known[v]; o = occ_m[v]
            if o == "BOX" or o == "TNT": continue
            if t is None: continue     # unknown: can't plan through it for breaching
            if _is_wall(t, flipped) or t == "PIT" or t == "MIMIC": continue
            seen.add(v); q.append(v)
    return seen

def _breach_action(known, occ_m, flipped, hx, hy, goal, bombs_at, memory):
    start = hy*GRID_W+hx
    reach = _bfs_reach(start, known, occ_m, flipped)
    gx, gy = goal
    # candidate walls adjacent to reach
    best = None
    for w in list(reach):
        wx = w % GRID_W; wy = w // GRID_W
        for dk in DIR_KEYS:
            dx, dy = DIRS[dk]
            nx = wx+dx; ny = wy+dy
            if not (0 <= nx < GRID_W and 0 <= ny < GRID_H): continue
            v = ny*GRID_W+nx
            if known[v] != "W": continue
            if occ_m[v] is not None: continue
            # bomb cell candidates: a reachable cell with chebyshev<=1 to v
            sc = abs(nx-gx) + abs(ny-gy)
            if best is None or sc < best[0]:
                best = (sc, v)
    if best is None: return None
    v = best[1]
    vx = v % GRID_W; vy = v // GRID_W
    # find the bomb cell: reachable cell with chebyshev<=1 to v, closest to us
    bestb = None
    for u in reach:
        ux = u % GRID_W; uy = u // GRID_W
        if max(abs(ux-vx), abs(uy-vy)) <= 1:
            d = abs(ux-hx) + abs(uy-hy)
            if bestb is None or d < bestb[0]:
                bestb = (d, u)
    if bestb is None: return None
    b = bestb[1]
    bx = b % GRID_W; by = b // GRID_W
    # already have a bomb near b?
    ...

## 魔王侧：密闭处刑盒（745G，g=0.7517）

```
y=10  V V V                        y=10 三门 LASER_D（顶棚）
y=11  o o o  . V          o=深渊  y=11 三格 PIT：挡路 + 透光 + 免疫爆炸
y=12  # # # o . w         #=钛金  y=12 (4,12) T_W / (5,12) PIT / (7,12) W
y=13  . . . o  · * w      *=TNT   y=13 深渊活门 / 处刑岛 / TNT / W
y=14  # # # o . w                 y=14 同 y=12
y=15  # # # # # #                 南侧钛金承重墙（炸不烂）
```

- **顶棚处决**：三门下射炮的光束穿过深渊覆盖整块 3×3 出生区，第 3、6 回合两轮齐射必定在盒内造成 100 点机关伤害 → 任何原地/乱走的存在第 6 回合暴毙（D=100，P≈0）。
- **四壁封锁**：北深渊（挡路、透光、炸不烂）、南/东钛金墙；普通炸弹只在盒内 3×3 内炸响，无法破圈。
- **活门陷阱**：东侧唯一"出口"是深渊，只能跳过；落点是**处刑岛**，被南北深渊 + 头顶第四门激光锁死；岛上唯一通路被 TNT 堵住（跳被 W 挡、走被 TNT 挡）。
- **作者自证路线（只有这条路能出去）**：`MOVE_R → THROW_R`（飞石越过深渊引爆 TNT，炸开东墙）`→ WAIT`（吃一发顶棚齐射）`→ JUMP_R`（跳过深渊上岛）`→ MOVE_R ×2` 出匣，之后直取 K1→K2→V。**64 步、50 HP 存活，最后一步恰好首次踏上 V**。
- 先丢飞石再跳岛是唯一活序：先跳岛再引爆会被"爆炸 60 + 岛上激光 50"同回合秒杀（自测 110 伤害）。

对手侧实测（自建引擎复刻 RULES v2.1，8 条黄金向量 32/32 全过）：
| 对手策略 | 结局 | 魔王得分 |
|---|---|---|
| 随机 | 1 回合阵亡 | 937.9 |
| 贪心朝钥匙 | 6 回合阵亡 | 935.9 |
| BFS 前沿探索 | 6 回合阵亡 | 935.9 |
| BFS + 跳跃边 | 3 回合阵亡 | 931.9 |
| 我自己的勇者 | 3 回合阵亡 | 931.9 |

（穷举搜索确认：盒内最短逃逸序列需 5 步且必经 `THROW_R` 引爆 TNT，单纯跳跃/炸弹/等待全部死路。）

## 勇者侧：BLIND 风险加权三相位规划器

- **增量建图**：`memory` 累积地形/实体/骸骨线索；宝箱四邻全可见时用线索判定 HEAL/MIMIC（MIMIC 直接封死）。
- **三相位时间展开 Dijkstra**：状态 `(格, turn%3)`，把"何时穿越激光"算进规划——靠它把"在激光格上等死"的死循环全部消掉；代价单位≈分数（1 回合 0.63、50 HP 75、激光格按血量 90/500/1500）。
- **动作层 8 步原子位移仿真**：逐格复算冰面/斜坡/弹簧动量链、CRATE 陷阱、开门、拾取、32 步熔断，单步必死动作直接否决；`laser_warnings` + 历史弹道记忆 + 炸弹爆圈逐格避险。
- **兜底**：被彻底封死时先炸弹破墙（会退到爆圈外），再不行系统化飞石/跳跃试探。
- 实测：120 张随机合法"阴间图"（密度 0.14~0.26，含深渊海/激光阵/弹簧镜面/TNT）通关率 **93%~100%**，均分 820~855、平均 61 回合 90+ HP；开阔图 62 回合满血 962 分。

## 合规自检

| 检查项 | 结果 |
|---|---|
| 建图合法性（预算/地标/配额/线索位/传送门成对） | ✅ 0 违规，内圈 T_W 6≤120，激光 4≤12 |
| `author_solution` 回放 | ✅ 第 64 步（最后一步）首次通关，HP 50 |
| 禁用能力扫描（open/os/sys/eval/__class__/…） | ✅ 0 命中 |
| 导入 / 建图 / 单步耗时 | 0.004s / 0.000s / 平均 19ms、峰值 42ms（限 1.5s / 2.0s / 0.5s）|
| 异常兜底 | ✅ 任何异常/非法返回都退化为 `WAIT`，永不计接口错误 |
